Generic HTTP 403 identifies a denied operation. It does not establish a required role. Unsupported endpoints, unavailable modules and missing portal context remain distinct. Earlier failed fallback attempts remain in the diagnostic history even when the final collection succeeds.

Field/UDF impact

Additional fields, explicitly custom fields and built-in fields are distinct. Form metadata can reveal a lower bound without proving a complete registry. Case-folded UDF labels are mapping candidates unless the documented Incident/Service duplicate condition is established. Larger declared lengths do not prove oversized stored values.

Interpretation notes

  • A completed list traversal covers only records visible to this API key. It does not establish organization-wide completeness or access to every module.
  • The scan is not a transactional snapshot. Source records may be added, changed, or removed while pagination and detail reads are in progress.
  • List and detail coverage are separate. Counts and examples apply to the examined evidence; sampled observations must not be extrapolated into exact totals.
  • Unknown and manual-confirmation items remain unresolved. This report does not assign an overall readiness score or approve a production migration.
  • These artifacts contain assessment metadata, record identifiers, counts, and documented rules. Raw API records, credentials, ticket subjects, attachment filenames, and request bodies are excluded by the assessment contract.
  • SYNTHETIC DEMONSTRATION ONLY. No actual ServiceDesk Plus instance was contacted.
  • Source-only assessment; no migration, configuration changes, attachment downloads, or arbitrary query execution are performed.
  • Unknown and manual-confirmation findings are unresolved, not passed checks.
  • Manual answers describe user-supplied requirements and have not been verified against the API or target tenant.
  • An API credential can see only its authorized scope; completed pagination does not certify organization-wide visibility.
  • Rule documentation was reviewed on 2026-10-05. Confirm target-tenant and migration-build behavior with ManageEngine.
  • Positive counts can be lower bounds; findings may concern overlapping records and must not be summed as unique impacted records.
  • Portal-specific findings retain portal IDs; matching numeric record IDs in different portals are not merged.
  • Global resources are counted once. Portal users/technicians are membership observations; Assets, Computers and CIs overlap and are not summed into a unique asset population.
  • Environment-wide confirmation topics are counted once; source impact and coverage remain portal-specific.
  • Field definitions, option/default measurements and allowed schema labels are retained; raw field values, options, defaults and script bodies are excluded.
  • Acceptance items are an evidence and decision register. The source scan cannot approve future Cloud migration behavior.
  • Integration-key expiration was not queried. No verified read-only expiration API is implemented; key metadata is not required for this assessment.

Environmentmigration_acceptanceQ1AGREEMENT_REQUIREDWhich documented limitations apply to our source environment and proposed target?Confirm supported source builds and the mapping of every selected source portal to a target service desk. Confirm the intended target topology, required modules and acceptance criteria.
Environmentmigration_acceptanceQ2AGREEMENT_REQUIREDHow should old records, CMDB, Releases, Projects and custom modules be handled?Confirm supported migration or retention routes for each portal/module, age group, CMDB relationship and Release/Project subresource. Choose required history retention, source archive access and ownership of data that needs a separate route.
Environmentmigration_acceptanceQ3AGREEMENT_REQUIREDWhat needs confirmation regarding final-delta migration?Confirm reconciliation of changed/deleted records, notes, attachments, approvals and tasks, plus target freshness and rollback behavior. Approve the freeze window, mail transition, delta rehearsal and rollback decision owner.
Environmentmigration_acceptanceQ4AGREEMENT_REQUIREDWhich identifiers and relationships require preservation or cross-reference?Confirm Change ID retention, per-portal ID cross-references, linked/dependent requests, request-change mappings and legacy links. Choose the cross-reference format and tests for each required relationship and historical link.
Environmentmigration_acceptanceQ5AGREEMENT_REQUIREDWhich settings, fields, workflows, scripts, reports or modules require reconstruction or licensing?Confirm field/type mapping, SLA and lifecycle/workflow matching, unsupported form actions, reporting paths, module licenses and discovery architecture. Assign owners to reconstruction and external integrations; agree which behaviors must be equivalent at go-live.
Environmentmigration_acceptanceQ6AGREEMENT_REQUIREDWhat export and recovery capabilities require confirmation?Obtain tenant-specific export completeness/frequency, recovery granularity, RPO/RTO, support involvement and exit commitments. Define retention, recovery objectives, periodic export ownership and when source retirement is permitted.
Environmentmigration_acceptanceQ7AGREEMENT_REQUIREDWhich failures or unresolved differences should block migration acceptance?Agree evidence requirements and exception handling for every unresolved migration difference. Approve go/no-go thresholds and accountable owners; resolve or explicitly accept each blocker before cutover.
